149:T69b,{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"FAQPage","mainEntity":[{"@type":"Question","name":"Is AwaitIO an Airplane alternative?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Yes, for teams where developers write the tools in TypeScript. Each Airplane task becomes an async function in your own repo, served by your own app."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Is Airplane still available?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"No. Airtable acquired Airplane in January 2024, and the product shut down on 1 March 2024."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Can I import my Airplane tasks into AwaitIO?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"There is no automatic import. A JavaScript task moves almost as it is: its parameters become await io.input calls and its body goes into the handler."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Can AwaitIO shut down my tools like Airplane did?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"No. AwaitIO runs inside your own app, on your own servers.
